- **primer-audit**: Auditor for the primer skill. - **privacy-opsec-suite**: This page is the complete command reference for the `privacy-opsec-suite` plugin. It carries one entry per command: how it works, why it is useful, when to reach for it, and what it hands off. Read it when you are picking an anonymity command or wiring one into a larger run. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/code-ops-docs/40%20Engineering/Handbook/commands/privacy-opsec-suite.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-anon-session-audit**: Use to verify sessions are unlinkable. Owns linkability and session identity, not network egress or file metadata. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-anon-session-audit.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-anonymity-threat-model**: Use for the keystone anonymity threat model the other privacy audits build on. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-anonymity-threat-model.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-authorship-hygiene**: Use when a commit, PR, or branch must carry no AI or tooling trace before publishing. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-authorship-hygiene.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-fingerprint-resistance**: Use to reduce the fingerprinting and uniqueness surface that could re-link anonymous users. Timing and size go to traffic-analysis-resistance. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-fingerprint-resistance.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-leak-incident-response**: Use when an anonymity or privacy leak is suspected: triage, contain, scope the blast radius, and plan remediation without making it worse. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-leak-incident-response.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-metadata-leak-audit**: Use to find PII or identifiers leaking in logs, telemetry, errors, headers, or file metadata. Timing and size go to traffic-analysis-resistance. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-metadata-leak-audit.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-hardening**: Use when a LEAK_REGISTER.md exists and its leaks need safe fixes, each pinned by a regression test. Requires a register. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-hardening.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-pr-gate**: Use as a pre-merge gate blocking egress, logging, identifier, fingerprint, correlation, or weaker anonymity defaults. Anonymity twin of /rigor-deep-review. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-pr-gate.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-doc-alignment**: Use when privacy promises, the threat model, or opsec runbooks have drifted from code; reconciles them into one source of truth. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-doc-alignment.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-feature-design**: Use to find and specify high-value privacy and trust features, each gated against the anonymity model. Discovery and specification only. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-feature-design.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-supply-chain-trust**: Use to vet dependencies for telemetry, phone-home, egress, CVEs, and build and lockfile integrity under an anonymity-hostile model. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-supply-chain-trust.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-tor-egress-audit**: Use when you need to prove no traffic escapes the proxy or Tor. Owns network egress and routing. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-tor-egress-audit.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-traffic-analysis-resistance**: Use to reduce timing, size, and volume side channels and add padding or batching defaults. Header and TLS fingerprints go to fingerprint-resistance. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-traffic-analysis-resistance.md))
